Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in asteroids theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 459 and the invariant I(A,B) = 862 mod 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to asteroids theory, we decompose the problem into 459 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 862. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
